# set_avg.F — 판독 구간 기록

구간은 1행부터 5800행까지 빈틈없이 이어진다.

| 구간 | 내용 |
|---|---|
| 1–32 | 1행 `#include "cppdefs.h"`는 cppdefs.h를 포함한다. 2행은 set_avg_mod 모듈을 연다. 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES` 안에서 시간 평균(time average)을 다음 시간 단계 시작에 누적한다는 지연 계산 주석과 저작권·라이선스를 적는다(5–15). 17–22행 전처리 분기의 주석은 조석 제거(detiding)에 필요한 최소제곱(least squares) 계수를 계산하며 일반 시간 평균이 필요하므로 set_detide를 마지막에 호출한다고 설명한다. implicit none·PRIVATE·PUBLIC set_avg·CONTAINS를 선언한다(25–30). 원문: `#include "cppdefs.h"` (1); `#ifdef AVERAGES` (3); `# if defined AVERAGES_DETIDE && (defined SSH_TIDES \|\| defined UV_TIDES)` (17); `# endif` (22). |
| 33–73 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·2행 set_avg_mod 안. set_avg(ng,tile)을 시작한다(33). WET_DRY일 때 mod_grid와 set_avg_masks를 가져온다. 조석 제거 조건은 mod_tides를 가져온다. ICE_MODEL과 SOLVE3D 조건은 ice_set_avg_tile을 가져온다(36–50). ng·tile은 입력 정수이다(54). MyFile은 __FILE__이며 tile.h를 포함한다(58–61). PROFILE은 wclock_on을 호출한다(63–65). set_avg_tile에는 타일(tile) 경계와 KOUT을 전달하며 SOLVE3D이면 NOUT도 전달한다(66–72). 원문: `SUBROUTINE set_avg (ng, tile)` (33); `# ifdef WET_DRY` (37); `# endif` (39); `# if defined AVERAGES_DETIDE && (defined SSH_TIDES \|\| defined UV_TIDES)` (41); `# endif` (43); `# if defined ICE_MODEL && defined SOLVE3D` (45); `# endif` (47); `# ifdef WET_DRY` (48); `# endif` (50); `character (len=*), parameter :: MyFile =                          &` (58); `&  __FILE__` (59); `# include "tile.h"` (61); `# ifdef PROFILE` (63); `CALL wclock_on (ng, iNLM, 5, __LINE__, MyFile)` (64); `# endif` (65); `CALL set_avg_tile (ng, tile, iNLM,                                &` (66); `&                   LBi, UBi, LBj, UBj,                            &` (67); `&                   IminS, ImaxS, JminS, JmaxS,                    &` (68); `# ifdef SOLVE3D` (69); `&                   NOUT,                                          &` (70); `# endif` (71); `&                   KOUT)` (72). |
| 74–117 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·33행 set_avg 안. 조석 제거 조건에서 set_detide_tile에 NTC·KOUT·조건부 NOUT과 CosOmega·SinOmega·평균 및 합계 배열·조화 성분 곱 배열을 전달한다(74–91). WET_DRY이면 set_avg_masks에 네 격자 마스크(mask)를 전달한다(93–101). ICE_MODEL과 SOLVE3D이면 ice_set_avg_tile에 IOUT을 전달한다(103–108). PROFILE이면 wclock_off를 호출한다(110–112). RETURN과 set_avg 종료가 있다(114–115). 원문: `# if defined AVERAGES_DETIDE && (defined SSH_TIDES \|\| defined UV_TIDES)` (74); `CALL set_detide_tile (ng, tile,                                   &` (75); `&                      LBi, UBi, LBj, UBj,                         &` (76); `&                      IminS, ImaxS, JminS, JmaxS,                 &` (77); `&                      NTC(ng), KOUT,                              &` (78); `#  ifdef SOLVE3D` (79); `&                      NOUT,                                       &` (80); `#  endif` (81); `&                      TIDES(ng) % CosOmega,                       &` (82); `&                      TIDES(ng) % SinOmega,                       &` (83); `&                      TIDES(ng) % CosW_avg,                       &` (84); | 118–199 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·2행 set_avg_mod 안. set_avg_tile의 입력은 ng·tile·model·배열 및 작업 경계·Kout이며 SOLVE3D이면 Nout을 추가한다(118–124·168–174). 조건에 따라 mod_coupling·mod_mixing·mod_bbl·mod_sedbed·mod_sediment를 가져온다(127–146). exchange_2d_mod·조건부 exchange_3d_mod와 mp_exchange2d/mp_exchange3d·uv_rotate2d/uv_rotate3d·vorticity_avg_tile을 가져온다(148–162). fac, 격자별 pfac/rfac/ufac/vfac, 조건부 3차원 잠재 와도(potential vorticity)·상대 와도(relative vorticity), 2차원 와도(vorticity), BBL_MODEL 작업 배열 wrk를 선언한다(179–196). set_bounds.h를 포함한다(198). 원문: `SUBROUTINE set_avg_tile (ng, tile, model,                         &` (118); `&                         LBi, UBi, LBj, UBj,                      &` (119); `&                         IminS, ImaxS, JminS, JmaxS,              &` (120); `# ifdef SOLVE3D` (121); `&                         Nout,                                    &` (122); `# endif` (123); `&                         Kout)` (124); `# if defined FORWARD_WRITE && defined SOLVE3D` (130); `# endif` (132); `# ifdef SOLVE3D` (135); `# endif` (137); `# if defined BBL_MODEL` (140); `# endif` (142); `# if defined SEDIMENT && defined BEDLOAD` (143); `# endif` (146); `# ifdef SOLVE3D` (149); `# endif` (151); `# ifdef DISTRIBUTE` (152); `#  ifdef SOLVE3D` (154); `#  endif` (156); `# endif` (157); `# ifdef SOLVE3D` (159); `# endif` (161); `# ifdef SOLVE3D` (172); `# endif` (174); `# ifdef SOLVE3D` (188); `# endif` (191); `# ifdef BBL_MODEL` (194); `# endif` (196); `# include "set_bounds.h"` (198). |

| 257–294 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·118행 set_avg_tile·252–255행 시간 평균 초기화 IF 안. WET_DRY에서 pmask_avg·rmask_avg·umask_avg·vmask_avg를 각 격자 범위의 full 마스크를 0.0_r8 이상 1.0_r8 이하로 제한하여 초기화한다(257–291). 네 DO 블록은 각각 Jstr/JstrR·Istr/IstrR를 사용한다. 상태 변수 초기화 주석이 이어진다(293–294). 원문: `# ifdef WET_DRY` (257); `DO j=Jstr,JendR` (263); `DO i=Istr,IendR` (264); `GRID(ng)%pmask_avg(i,j)=MAX(0.0_r8,                         &` (265); `&                                  MIN(GRID(ng)%pmask_full(i,j),   &` (266); `&                                      1.0_r8))` (267); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (270); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (271); `GRID(ng)%rmask_avg(i,j)=MAX(0.0_r8,                         &` (272); `&                                  MIN(GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j),   &` (273); `&                                      1.0_r8))` (274); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (277); `DO i=Istr,IendR` (278); `GRID(ng)%umask_avg(i,j)=MAX(0.0_r8,                         &` (279); `&                                  MIN(GRID(ng)%umask_full(i,j),   &` (280); `&                                      1.0_r8))` (281); `DO j=Jstr,JendR` (284); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (285); `GRID(ng)%vmask_avg(i,j)=MAX(0.0_r8,                         &` (286); `&                                  MIN(GRID(ng)%vmask_full(i,j),   &` (287); `&                                      1.0_r8))` (288); `# endif` (291). |
| 295–343 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·118행 set_avg_tile·252–255행 초기화 IF 안. Aout 선택별로 자유수면(free surface)·수심 평균 속도(depth averaged velocity)의 Kout 값을 avgzeta·avgu2d·avgv2d에 복사한다(295–328). WET_DRY에서는 각각 rmask_full·umask_full·vmask_full을 곱한다. 동쪽·북쪽 2차원 속도 출력이 모두 선택되면 uv_rotate2d를 .FALSE., .FALSE. 인수로 호출한다(330–342). WET_DRY에서 두 배열 모두 vmask_full을 곱한다(377–380·390–393). 원문: `IF (Aout(idu3dE,ng)) THEN` (372); `DO k=1,N(ng)` (373); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (374); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (375); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgu3dE(i,j,k)=OCEAN(ng)%ua(i,j,k)` (376); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (377); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgu3dE(i,j,k)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgu3dE(i,j,k)*  &` (378); `&                                     GRID(ng)%vmask_full(i,j)` (379); `#  endif` (380); `IF (Aout(idv3dN,ng)) THEN` (385); `DO k=1,N(ng)` (386); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (387); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (388); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgv3dN(i,j,k)=OCEAN(ng)%va(i,j,k)` (389); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (390); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgv3dN(i,j,k)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgv3dN(i,j,k)*  &` (391); `&                                     GRID(ng)%vmask_full(i,j)` (392); `#  endif` (393). |
| 399–456 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·344행 `# ifdef SOLVE3D`·118행 set_avg_tile·252–255행 초기화 IF 안. 수직 좌표 방향 유량 OCEAN%W에 pm·pn을 곱하여 avgw3d를 초기화한다(399–413). 실제 수직 속도(vertical velocity) wvel은 avgwvel에 복사한다(414–426). 두 수직 배열은 k=0..N(ng)이며 WET_DRY에서 rmask_full을 곱한다. 밀도 이상(density anomaly) rho는 k=1..N(ng)에서 avgrho로 복사한다(428–440). it=1..NT(ng)에서 선택된 추적자(tracer)의 Nout 값을 avgt로 복사하고 rmask_full을 곱한다(441–455). 원문: `IF (Aout(idOvel,ng)) THEN` (399); `DO k=0,N(ng)` (400); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (401); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (402); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgw3d(i,j,k)=OCEAN(ng)%W(i,j,k)*           &` (403); `&                                    GRID(ng)%pm(i,j)*             &` (404); `&                                    GRID(ng)%pn(i,j)` (405); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (406); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgw3d(i,j,k)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgw3d(i,j,k)*    &` (407); `&                                    GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (408); `#  endif` (409); `IF (Aout(idWvel,ng)) THEN` (414); `DO k=0,N(ng)` (415); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (416); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (417); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgwvel(i,j,k)=OCEAN(ng)%wvel(i,j,k)` (418); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (419); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgwvel(i,j,k)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgwvel(i,j,k)*  &` (420); `&                                     GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (421); `#  endif` (422); `IF (Aout(idDano,ng)) THEN` (428); `DO k=1,N(ng)` (429); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (430); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (431); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgrho(i,j,k)=OCEAN(ng)%rho(i,j,k)` (432); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (433); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgrho(i,j,k)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgrho(i,j,k)*    &` (434); `&                                    GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (435); `#  endif` (436); `DO it=1,NT(ng)` (441); `IF (Aout(idTvar(it),ng)) THEN` (442); `DO k=1,N(ng)` (443); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (444); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (445); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgt(i,j,k,it)=OCEAN(ng)%t(i,j,k,Nout,it)` (446); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (447); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgt(i,j,k,it)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgt(i,j,k,it)*&` (448); `&                                       GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (449); `#  endif` (450). |
| 457–484 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·344행 `# ifdef SOLVE3D`·118행 set_avg_tile·252–255행 초기화 IF 안. SEDIMENT와 BEDLOAD를 함께 요구한다(457). 퇴적물(sediment) 종류 it=1..NST에서 idUbld/ idVbld 출력 선택에 따라 소류사(bedload) bedldu/bedldv를 avgbedldu/avgbedldv로 복사한다(458–482). WET_DRY이면 각각 umask_full·vmask_full을 곱한다. 483행은 퇴적물 전처리 분기를 닫는다. 원문: `#  if defined SEDIMENT && defined BEDLOAD` (457); `DO it=1,NST` (458); `IF (Aout(idUbld(it),ng)) THEN` (459); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (460); `DO i=Istr,IendR` (461); `SEDBED(ng)%avgbedldu(i,j,it)=SEDBED(ng)%bedldu(i,j,it)` (462); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (463); `SEDBED(ng)%avgbedldu(i,j,it)=SEDBED(ng)%bedldu(i,j,it)* &` (464); `&                                       GRID(ng)%umask_full(i,j)` (465); `#   endif` (466); `IF (Aout(idVbld(it),ng)) THEN` (471); `DO j=Jstr,JendR` (472); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (473); `SEDBED(ng)%avgbedldv(i,j,it)=SEDBED(ng)%bedldv(i,j,it)` (474); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (475); `SEDBED(ng)%avgbedldv(i,j,it)=SEDBED(ng)%bedldv(i,j,it)* &` (476); `&                                       GRID(ng)%vmask_full(i,j)` (477); `#   endif` (478); `#  endif` (483). |
| 485–527 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·344행 `# ifdef SOLVE3D`·118행 set_avg_tile·252–255행 초기화 IF 안. LMD_MIXING·MY25_MIXING·GLS_MIXING 중 하나가 정의되면 출력 선택에 따라 수직 점성(vertical viscosity) Akv와 수온 추적자 확산계수(diffusivity) Akt(:,:, :,itemp)를 avgAKv·avgAKt로 복사한다(485–511). SALINITY이면 isalt 성분을 avgAKs로 복사한다(512–526). 세 블록은 k=0..N(ng), JstrR:JendR·IstrR:IendR를 순회하며 WET_DRY일 때 rmask_full을 곱한다. 527행은 혼합 전처리 조건을 닫는다. 원문: `#  if defined LMD_MIXING \|\| defined MY25_MIXING \|\| defined GLS_MIXING` (485); `IF (Aout(idVvis,ng)) THEN` (486); `DO k=0,N(ng)` (487); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (488); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (489); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgAKv(i,j,k)=MIXING(ng)%Akv(i,j,k)` (490); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (491); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgAKv(i,j,k)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgAKv(i,j,k)*    &` (492); `&                                    GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (493); `#   endif` (494); `IF (Aout(idTdif,ng)) THEN` (499); `DO k=0,N(ng)` (500); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (501); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (502); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgAKt(i,j,k)=MIXING(ng)%Akt(i,j,k,itemp)` (503); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (504); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgAKt(i,j,k)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgAKt(i,j,k)*    &` (505); `&                                    GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (506); `#   endif` (507); `#   ifdef SALINITY` (512); `IF (Aout(idSdif,ng)) THEN` (513); `DO k=0,N(ng)` (514); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (515); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (516); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgAKs(i,j,k)=MIXING(ng)%Akt(i,j,k,isalt)` (517); `#    ifdef WET_DRY` (518); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgAKs(i,j,k)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgAKs(i,j,k)*    &` (519); `&                                    GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (520); `#    endif` (521); `#   endif` (526); `#  endif` (527). |
| 528–555 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·344행 `# ifdef SOLVE3D`·118행 set_avg_tile·252–255행 초기화 IF 안. LMD_SKPP에서 출력 선택 idHsbl에 따라 hsbl을 avghsbl로 복사한다(528–540). LMD_BKPP에서 idHbbl에 따라 hbbl을 avghbbl로 복사한다(541–553). 두 경계층(boundary layer) 깊이 배열은 rho 위치 범위에서 초기화하고 WET_DRY이면 rmask_full을 곱한다. 554행은 344행 SOLVE3D를 닫는다. 원문: `#  ifdef LMD_SKPP` (528); `IF (Aout(idHsbl,ng)) THEN` (529); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (530); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (531); `AVERAGE(ng)%avghsbl(i,j)=MIXING(ng)%hsbl(i,j)` (532); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (533); `AVERAGE(ng)%avghsbl(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avghsbl(i,j)*        &` (534); `&                                 GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (535); `#   endif` (536); `#  endif` (540); `#  ifdef LMD_BKPP` (541); `IF (Aout(idHbbl,ng)) THEN` (542); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (543); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (544); `AVERAGE(ng)%avghbbl(i,j)=MIXING(ng)%hbbl(i,j)` (545); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (546); `AVERAGE(ng)%avghbbl(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avghbbl(i,j)*        &` (547); `&                                 GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (548); `#   endif` (549); `#  endif` (553); `# endif` (554). |
| 556–608 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·118행 set_avg_tile·252–255행 초기화 IF 안. FORWARD_WRITE와 SOLVE3D가 함께 정의될 때 2차원·3차원 결합(coupling) 항 DU_avg1·DU_avg2·DV_avg1·DV_avg2를 각각 출력 선택에 따라 avgDU_avg1·avgDU_avg2·avgDV_avg1·avgDV_avg2로 복사한다(556–605). WET_DRY에서는 DU 항에 umask_full, DV 항에 vmask_full을 곱한다. 표면·바닥 플럭스(flux) 초기화 주석이 이어진다(607–608). 원문: `# if defined FORWARD_WRITE && defined SOLVE3D` (556); `IF (Aout(idUfx1,ng)) THEN` (560); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (561); `DO i=Istr,IendR` (562); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgDU_avg1(i,j)=COUPLING(ng)%DU_avg1(i,j)` (563); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (564); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgDU_avg1(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgDU_avg1(i,j)*  &` (565); `&                                    GRID(ng)%umask_full(i,j)` (566); `#  endif` (567); `IF (Aout(idUfx2,ng)) THEN` (571); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (572); `DO i=Istr,IendR` (573); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgDU_avg2(i,j)=COUPLING(ng)%DU_avg2(i,j)` (574); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (575); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgDU_avg2(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgDU_avg2(i,j)*  &` (576); `&                                    GRID(ng)%umask_full(i,j)` (577); `#  endif` (578); `IF (Aout(idVfx1,ng)) THEN` (583); `DO j=Jstr,JendR` (584); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (585); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgDV_avg1(i,j)=COUPLING(ng)%DV_avg1(i,j)` (586); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (587); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgDV_avg1(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgDV_avg1(i,j)*  &` (588); `&                                    GRID(ng)%vmask_full(i,j)` (589); `#  endif` (590); `IF (Aout(idVfx2,ng)) THEN` (594); `DO j=Jstr,JendR` (595); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (596); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgDV_avg2(i,j)=COUPLING(ng)%DV_avg2(i,j)` (597); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (598); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgDV_avg2(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgDV_avg2(i,j)*  &` (599); `&                                    GRID(ng)%vmask_full(i,j)` (600); `#  endif` (601); `# endif` (605). |
| 609–653 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·118행 set_avg_tile·252–255행 초기화 IF 안. 출력 선택 idUsms·idVsms·idUbms·idVbms별로 표면 응력(surface stress) sustr/svstr와 바닥 응력(bottom stress) bustr/bvstr를 avgsus/avgsvs·avgbus/avgbvs로 복사한다(609–653). u 성분은 JstrR:JendR·Istr:IendR, v 성분은 Jstr:JendR·IstrR:IendR를 순회한다. WET_DRY는 해당 u/v full 마스크를 곱한다. 원문: `IF (Aout(idUsms,ng)) THEN` (609); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (610); `DO i=Istr,IendR` (611); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgsus(i,j)=FORCES(ng)%sustr(i,j)` (612); `# ifdef WET_DRY` (613); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgsus(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgsus(i,j)*          &` (614); `&                                GRID(ng)%umask_full(i,j)` (615); `# endif` (616); `IF (Aout(idVsms,ng)) THEN` (620); `DO j=Jstr,JendR` (621); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (622); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgsvs(i,j)=FORCES(ng)%svstr(i,j)` (623); `# ifdef WET_DRY` (624); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgsvs(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgsvs(i,j)*          &` (625); `&                                GRID(ng)%vmask_full(i,j)` (626); `# endif` (627); `IF (Aout(idUbms,ng)) THEN` (632); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (633); `DO i=Istr,IendR` (634); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgbus(i,j)=FORCES(ng)%bustr(i,j)` (635); `# ifdef WET_DRY` (636); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgbus(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgbus(i,j)*          &` (637); `&                                GRID(ng)%umask_full(i,j)` (638); `# endif` (639); `IF (Aout(idVbms,ng)) THEN` (643); `DO j=Jstr,JendR` (644); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (645); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgbvs(i,j)=FORCES(ng)%bvstr(i,j)` (646); `# ifdef WET_DRY` (647); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgbvs(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgbvs(i,j)*          &` (648); `&                                GRID(ng)%vmask_full(i,j)` (649); `# endif` (650). |

`IF (Aout(idUbrs,ng)) THEN` (655); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (656); `DO i=Istr,IendR` (657); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgUbrs(i,j)=BBL(ng)%bustrc(i,j)` (658); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (659); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgUbrs(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgUbrs(i,j)*        &` (660); `&                                 GRID(ng)%umask_full(i,j)` (661); `#  endif` (662); `IF (Aout(idVbrs,ng)) THEN` (666); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (667); `DO i=Istr,IendR` (668); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgVbrs(i,j)=BBL(ng)%bvstrc(i,j)` (669); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (670); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgVbrs(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgVbrs(i,j)*        &` (671); `&                                 GRID(ng)%umask_full(i,j)` (672); `#  endif` (673); `IF (Aout(idUbws,ng)) THEN` (677); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (678); `DO i=Istr,IendR` (679); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgUbws(i,j)=BBL(ng)%bustrw(i,j)` (680); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (681); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgUbws(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgUbws(i,j)*        &` (682); `&                                 GRID(ng)%umask_full(i,j)` (683); `#  endif` (684); `IF (Aout(idVbws,ng)) THEN` (688); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (689); `DO i=Istr,IendR` (690); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgVbws(i,j)=BBL(ng)%bvstrw(i,j)` (691); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (692); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgVbws(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgVbws(i,j)*        &` (693); `&                                 GRID(ng)%umask_full(i,j)` (694); `#  endif` (695); `IF (Aout(idUbcs,ng)) THEN` (699); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (700); `DO i=Istr,IendR` (701); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgUbcs(i,j)=BBL(ng)%bustrcwmax(i,j)` (702); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (703); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgUbcs(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgUbcs(i,j)*        &` (704); `&                                 GRID(ng)%umask_full(i,j)` (705); `#  endif` (706); `IF (Aout(idVbcs,ng)) THEN` (710); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (711); `DO i=Istr,IendR` (712); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgVbcs(i,j)=BBL(ng)%bvstrcwmax(i,j)` (713); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (714); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgVbcs(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgVbcs(i,j)*        &` (715); `&                                 GRID(ng)%umask_full(i,j)` (716); `#  endif` (717). |
| 721–781 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·654행 `# ifdef BBL_MODEL`·118행 set_avg_tile·252–255행 초기화 IF 안. idUVwc 선택이면 wrk를 LBi:UBi·LBj:UBj로 할당하고 0으로 채운 뒤 두 최대 복합 바닥 응력 성분의 제곱합에 1.0E-10_r8을 더하여 제곱근을 계산한다(721–725). avgUVwc로 복사한 뒤 wrk를 해제한다(726–736). Ubot·Vbot·Ur·Vr는 각 선택에 따라 avgUbot·avgVbot·avgUbur·avgVbvr에 복사한다(737–780). 이 다섯 평균 블록은 WET_DRY에서 umask_full을 곱한다. 781행은 BBL_MODEL을 닫는다. 원문: `IF (Aout(idUVwc,ng)) THEN` (721); `wrk(LBi:UBi,LBj:UBj)=0.0_r8` (723); `wrk=sqrt(BBL(ng)%bustrcwmax*BBL(ng)%bustrcwmax+               &` (724); `&             BBL(ng)%bvstrcwmax*BBL(ng)%bvstrcwmax+1.0E-10_r8)` (725); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (726); `DO i=Istr,IendR` (727); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgUVwc(i,j)=wrk(i,j)` (728); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (729); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgUVwc(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgUVwc(i,j)*        &` (730); `&                                 GRID(ng)%umask_full(i,j)` (731); `#  endif` (732); `IF (Aout(idUbot,ng)) THEN` (737); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (738); `DO i=Istr,IendR` (739); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgUbot(i,j)=BBL(ng)%Ubot(i,j)` (740); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (741); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgUbot(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgUbot(i,j)*        &` (742); `&                                 GRID(ng)%umask_full(i,j)` (743); `#  endif` (744); `IF (Aout(idVbot,ng)) THEN` (748); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (749); `DO i=Istr,IendR` (750); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgVbot(i,j)=BBL(ng)%Vbot(i,j)` (751); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (752); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgVbot(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgVbot(i,j)*        &` (753); `&                                 GRID(ng)%umask_full(i,j)` (754); `#  endif` (755); `IF (Aout(idUbur,ng)) THEN` (759); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (760); `DO i=Istr,IendR` (761); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgUbur(i,j)=BBL(ng)%Ur(i,j)` (762); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (763); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgUbur(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgUbur(i,j)*        &` (764); `&                                 GRID(ng)%umask_full(i,j)` (765); `#  endif` (766); `IF (Aout(idVbvr,ng)) THEN` (770); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (771); `DO i=Istr,IendR` (772); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgVbvr(i,j)=BBL(ng)%Vr(i,j)` (773); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (774); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgVbvr(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgVbvr(i,j)*        &` (775); `&                                 GRID(ng)%umask_full(i,j)` (776); `#  endif` (777); `# endif` (781). |
| 782–795 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·118행 set_avg_tile·252–255행 초기화 IF 안. 782행 SOLVE3D 아래에서 BULK_FLUXES·ECOSIM·ATM_PRESS 중 하나가 정의되고 idPair가 선택되면 대기압(atmospheric pressure) Pair를 avgPair에 복사한다(783–794). rho 위치 범위에서 WET_DRY이면 rmask_full을 곱한다(785–791). 795행은 세 대기 조건의 전처리 분기를 닫으며 SOLVE3D는 열려 있다. 원문: `# ifdef SOLVE3D` (782); `#  if defined BULK_FLUXES \|\| defined ECOSIM \|\| defined ATM_PRESS` (783); `IF (Aout(idPair,ng)) THEN` (784); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (785); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (786); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgPair(i,j)=FORCES(ng)%Pair(i,j)` (787); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (788); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgPair(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgPair(i,j)*        &` (789); `&                                 GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (790); `#   endif` (791); `#  endif` (795). |
| 796–848 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·782행 `# ifdef SOLVE3D`·118행 set_avg_tile·252–255행 초기화 IF 안. BULK_FLUXES에서 idTair가 선택되면 Tair를 avgTair로 복사한다(796–808). BULK_FLUXES 또는 ECOSIM이면 idUair·idVair별로 Uwind·Vwind를 avgUwind·avgVwind로 복사한다(809–832). 세 배열은 WET_DRY에서 rmask_full을 곱한다. 동쪽·북쪽 바람(wind) 출력이 모두 선택되면 uv_rotate2d(.FALSE., .FALSE.)를 호출하여 avgUwindE·avgVwindN을 채운다(834–846). MASKING에서는 rmask_full을 전달한다(839–841). 847행은 BULK_FLUXES 또는 ECOSIM 조건을 닫는다. 원문: `#  if defined BULK_FLUXES` (796); `IF (Aout(idTair,ng)) THEN` (797); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (798); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (799); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgTair(i,j)=FORCES(ng)%Tair(i,j)` (800); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (801); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgTair(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgTair(i,j)*        &` (802); `&                                 GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (803); `#   endif` (804); `#  endif` (808); `#  if defined BULK_FLUXES \|\| defined ECOSIM` (809); `IF (Aout(idUair,ng)) THEN` (810); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (811); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (812); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgUwind(i,j)=FORCES(ng)%Uwind(i,j)` (813); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (814); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgUwind(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgUwind(i,j)*      &` (815); `&                                  GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (816); `#   endif` (817); `IF (Aout(idVair,ng)) THEN` (822); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (823); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (824); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgVwind(i,j)=FORCES(ng)%Vwind(i,j)` (825); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (826); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgVwind(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgVwind(i,j)*      &` (827); `&                                  GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (828); `#   endif` (829); `IF (Aout(idUaiE,ng).and.Aout(idVaiN,ng)) THEN` (834); `CALL uv_rotate2d (ng, tile, .FALSE., .FALSE.,                 &` (835); `&                      LBi, UBi, LBj, UBj,                         &` (836); `&                      GRID(ng) % CosAngler,                       &` (837); `&                      GRID(ng) % SinAngler,                       &` (838); `#   ifdef MASKING` (839); `&                      GRID(ng)%rmask_full,                        &` (840); `#   endif` (841); `&                      FORCES(ng) % Uwind,                         &` (842); `&                      FORCES(ng) % Vwind,                         &` (843); `&                      AVERAGE(ng)%avgUwindE,                      &` (844); `&                      AVERAGE(ng)%avgVwindN)` (845); `#  endif` (847). |
| 849–886 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·782행 `# ifdef SOLVE3D`·118행 set_avg_tile·252–255행 초기화 IF 안. 표면 수온 추적자 플럭스 stflx(:,:,itemp)를 avgstf로 복사한다(849–859). SALINITY에서 stflx(:,:,isalt)를 avgswf로 복사한다(860–872). SHORTWAVE에서 단파 복사(shortwave radiation) srflx를 avgsrf로 복사한다(873–885). 각 출력 선택별 블록은 rho 위치 범위를 순회하며 WET_DRY이면 rmask_full을 곱한다. 원문: `IF (Aout(idTsur(itemp),ng)) THEN` (849); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (850); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (851); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgstf(i,j)=FORCES(ng)%stflx(i,j,itemp)` (852); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (853); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgstf(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgstf(i,j)*          &` (854); `&                                GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (855); `#  endif` (856); `#  ifdef SALINITY` (860); `IF (Aout(idTsur(isalt),ng)) THEN` (861); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (862); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (863); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgswf(i,j)=FORCES(ng)%stflx(i,j,isalt)` (864); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (865); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgswf(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgswf(i,j)*          &` (866); `&                                GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (867); `#   endif` (868); `#  endif` (872); `#  ifdef SHORTWAVE` (873); `IF (Aout(idSrad,ng)) THEN` (874); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (875); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (876); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgsrf(i,j)=FORCES(ng)%srflx(i,j)` (877); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (878); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgsrf(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgsrf(i,j)*          &` (879); `&                                GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (880); `#   endif` (881); `#  endif` (885). |
| 887–950 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·782행 `# ifdef SOLVE3D`·118행 set_avg_tile·252–255행 초기화 IF 안. BULK_FLUXES 또는 FRC_COUPLING에서 잠열(latent heat) lhflx·장파 복사(longwave radiation) lrflx·현열(sensible heat) shflx를 avglhf·avglrf·avgshf로 복사한다(887–923). BULK_FLUXES와 EMINUSP가 모두 정의되면 증발(evaporation) evap·강우(rain) rain을 avgevap·avgrain으로 복사한다(925–949). 모든 블록은 해당 Aout 선택을 검사하고 WET_DRY에서 rmask_full을 곱한다. 950행은 782행 SOLVE3D를 닫는다. 원문: `#  if defined BULK_FLUXES \|\| defined FRC_COUPLING` (887); `IF (Aout(idLhea,ng)) THEN` (888); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (889); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (890); `AVERAGE(ng)%avglhf(i,j)=FORCES(ng)%lhflx(i,j)` (891); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (892); `AVERAGE(ng)%avglhf(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avglhf(i,j)*          &` (893); `&                                GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (894); `#   endif` (895); `IF (Aout(idLrad,ng)) THEN` (900); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (901); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (902); `AVERAGE(ng)%avglrf(i,j)=FORCES(ng)%lrflx(i,j)` (903); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (904); `AVERAGE(ng)%avglrf(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avglrf(i,j)*          &` (905); `&                                GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (906); `#   endif` (907); `IF (Aout(idShea,ng)) THEN` (912); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (913); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (914); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgshf(i,j)=FORCES(ng)%shflx(i,j)` (915); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (916); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgshf(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgshf(i,j)*          &` (917); `&                                GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (918); `#   endif` (919); `#  endif` (923); `#  if defined BULK_FLUXES && defined EMINUSP` (925); `IF (Aout(idevap,ng)) THEN` (926); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (927); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (928); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgevap(i,j)=FORCES(ng)%evap(i,j)` (929); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (930); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgevap(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgevap(i,j)*        &` (931); `&                                 GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (932); `#   endif` (933); `IF (Aout(idrain,ng)) THEN` (938); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (939); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (940); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgrain(i,j)=FORCES(ng)%rain(i,j)` (941); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (942); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgrain(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgrain(i,j)*        &` (943); `&                                 GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (944); `#   endif` (945); `#  endif` (949); `# endif` (950). |
| 951–1000 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·118행 set_avg_tile·252–255행 초기화 IF 안. WEC에서 파랑의 흐름 영향(waves effect on currents) 필드를 초기화한다(951–954). idU2Sd·idV2Sd별로 수심 평균 Stokes 표류(Stokes drift) ubar_stokes·vbar_stokes를 avgu2Sd·avgv2Sd에 복사한다(955–976). idU2rs·idV2rs별로 rustr2d·rvstr2d를 avgu2rs·avgv2rs에 복사한다(978–999). u 성분에는 umask_full, v 성분에는 vmask_full을 WET_DRY에서 곱한다. 1000행은 WEC를 닫는다. 원문: `# ifdef WEC` (951); `IF (Aout(idU2Sd,ng)) THEN` (955); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (956); `DO i=Istr,IendR` (957); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgu2Sd(i,j)=OCEAN(ng)%ubar_stokes(i,j)` (958); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (959); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgu2Sd(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgu2Sd(i,j)*        &` (960); `&                                 GRID(ng)%umask_full(i,j)` (961); `#  endif` (962); `IF (Aout(idV2Sd,ng)) THEN` (966); `DO j=Jstr,JendR` (967); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (968); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgv2Sd(i,j)=OCEAN(ng)%vbar_stokes(i,j)` (969); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (970); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgv2Sd(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgv2Sd(i,j)*        &` (971); `&                                 GRID(ng)%vmask_full(i,j)` (972); `#  endif` (973); `IF (Aout(idU2rs,ng)) THEN` (978); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (979); `DO i=Istr,IendR` (980); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgu2rs(i,j)=MIXING(ng)%rustr2d(i,j)` (981); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (982); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgu2rs(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgu2rs(i,j)*        &` (983); `&                                 GRID(ng)%umask_full(i,j)` (984); `#  endif` (985); `IF (Aout(idV2rs,ng)) THEN` (989); `DO j=Jstr,JendR` (990); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (991); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgv2rs(i,j)=MIXING(ng)%rvstr2d(i,j)` (992); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (993); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgv2rs(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgv2rs(i,j)*        &` (994); `&                                 GRID(ng)%vmask_full(i,j)` (995); `#  endif` (996); `# endif` (1000). |
| 1001–1054 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·118행 set_avg_tile·252–255행 초기화 IF 안. SOLVE3D와 WEC에서 3차원 Stokes 성분 u_stokes·v_stokes·W_stokes·wstvel를 각 출력 선택에 따라 avgu3Sd·avgv3Sd·avgw3Sd·avgw3St로 복사한다(1001–1054). 모두 k=1..N(ng)을 사용한다. u 성분은 u 위치 및 umask_full을 사용한다. 나머지 세 성분은 Jstr:JendR·IstrR:IendR 및 vmask_full을 WET_DRY에서 사용한다. 원문: `# ifdef SOLVE3D` (1001); `#  ifdef WEC` (1002); `IF (Aout(idU3Sd,ng)) THEN` (1003); `DO k=1,N(ng)` (1004); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1005); `DO i=Istr,IendR` (1006); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgu3Sd(i,j,k)=OCEAN(ng)%u_stokes(i,j,k)` (1007); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (1008); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgu3Sd(i,j,k)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgu3Sd(i,j,k)*  &` (1009); `&                                     GRID(ng)%umask_full(i,j)` (1010); `#   endif` (1011); `IF (Aout(idV3Sd,ng)) THEN` (1016); `DO k=1,N(ng)` (1017); `DO j=Jstr,JendR` (1018); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1019); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgv3Sd(i,j,k)=OCEAN(ng)%v_stokes(i,j,k)` (1020); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (1021); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgv3Sd(i,j,k)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgv3Sd(i,j,k)*  &` (1022); `&                                     GRID(ng)%vmask_full(i,j)` (1023); `#   endif` (1024); `IF (Aout(idW3Sd,ng)) THEN` (1029); `DO k=1,N(ng)` (1030); `DO j=Jstr,JendR` (1031); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1032); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgw3Sd(i,j,k)=OCEAN(ng)%W_stokes(i,j,k)` (1033); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (1034); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgw3Sd(i,j,k)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgw3Sd(i,j,k)*  &` (1035); `&                                     GRID(ng)%vmask_full(i,j)` (1036); `#   endif` (1037); `IF (Aout(idW3St,ng)) THEN` (1042); `DO k=1,N(ng)` (1043); `DO j=Jstr,JendR` (1044); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1045); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgw3St(i,j,k)=OCEAN(ng)%wstvel(i,j,k)` (1046); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (1047); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgw3St(i,j,k)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgw3St(i,j,k)*  &` (1048); `&                                     GRID(ng)%vmask_full(i,j)` (1049); `#   endif` (1050). |
| 1055–1117 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·1001행 `# ifdef SOLVE3D`·1002행 `#  ifdef WEC`·118행 set_avg_tile·252–255행 초기화 IF 안. rustr3d·rvstr3d를 avgu3rs·avgv3rs로 복사하며 k=1..N(ng), WET_DRY에서 해당 u/v full 마스크를 사용한다(1055–1080). 1081행에서 WEC를 닫는다. WEC_VF에서 zetaw·qsp·bh를 출력 선택별로 avgWztw·avgwqsp·avgwbeh에 복사한다(1082–1116). 이 세 배열은 rho 위치 및 rmask_full을 사용한다. 1117행은 SOLVE3D를 닫는다. 원문: `IF (Aout(idU3rs,ng)) THEN` (1055); `DO k=1,N(ng)` (1056); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1057); `DO i=Istr,IendR` (1058); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgu3rs(i,j,k)=MIXING(ng)%rustr3d(i,j,k)` (1059); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (1060); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgu3rs(i,j,k)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgu3rs(i,j,k)*  &` (1061); `&                                     GRID(ng)%umask_full(i,j)` (1062); `#   endif` (1063); `IF (Aout(idV3rs,ng)) THEN` (1068); `DO k=1,N(ng)` (1069); `DO j=Jstr,JendR` (1070); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1071); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgv3rs(i,j,k)=MIXING(ng)%rvstr3d(i,j,k)` (1072); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (1073); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgv3rs(i,j,k)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgv3rs(i,j,k)*  &` (1074); `&                                     GRID(ng)%vmask_full(i,j)` (1075); `#   endif` (1076); `#  endif` (1081); `#  ifdef WEC_VF` (1082); `IF (Aout(idWztw,ng)) THEN` (1083); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1084); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1085); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgWztw(i,j)=OCEAN(ng)%zetaw(i,j)` (1086); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (1087); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgWztw(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgWztw(i,j)*        &` (1088); `&                                 GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (1089); `#   endif` (1090); `IF (Aout(idWqsp,ng)) THEN` (1094); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1095); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1096); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgwqsp(i,j)=OCEAN(ng)%qsp(i,j)` (1097); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (1098); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgwqsp(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgwqsp(i,j)*        &` (1099); `&                                 GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (1100); `#   endif` (1101); `IF (Aout(idWbeh,ng)) THEN` (1105); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1106); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1107); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgwbeh(i,j)=OCEAN(ng)%bh(i,j)` (1108); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (1109); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgwbeh(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgwbeh(i,j)*        &` (1110); `&                                 GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (1111); `#   endif` (1112); `#  endif` (1116); `# endif` (1117). |
| 1118–1155 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·118행 set_avg_tile·252–255행 초기화 IF 안. WAVES_HEIGHT에서 Hwave를 avgWamp로 복사하고 Hwave의 제곱을 avgWam2로 초기화한다(1118–1142). WAVES_LENGTH에서 Lwave를 avgWlen으로 복사한다(1143–1155). 세 블록은 각각 Aout 선택을 검사하고 rho 위치에서 WET_DRY이면 rmask_full을 곱한다. 원문: `# ifdef WAVES_HEIGHT` (1118); `IF (Aout(idWamp,ng)) THEN` (1119); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1120); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1121); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgWamp(i,j)=FORCES(ng)%Hwave(i,j)` (1122); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (1123); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgWamp(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgWamp(i,j)*        &` (1124); `&                                 GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (1125); `#  endif` (1126); `IF (Aout(idWam2,ng)) THEN` (1130); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1131); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1132); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgWam2(i,j)=FORCES(ng)%Hwave(i,j)*           &` (1133); `&                                 FORCES(ng)%Hwave(i,j)` (1134); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (1135); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgWam2(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgWam2(i,j)*        &` (1136); `&                                 GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (1137); `#  endif` (1138); `# endif` (1142); `# ifdef WAVES_LENGTH` (1143); `IF (Aout(idWlen,ng)) THEN` (1144); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1145); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1146); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgWlen(i,j)=FORCES(ng)%Lwave(i,j)` (1147); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (1148); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgWlen(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgWlen(i,j)*        &` (1149); `&                                 GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (1150); `#  endif` (1151); `# endif` (1155). |
| 1156–1194 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·118행 set_avg_tile·252–255행 초기화 IF 안. WAVES_LENGTHP에서 Lwavep를 avgWlep로 복사한다(1156–1168). WAVES_DIR에서 Dwave를 avgWdir로 복사한다(1169–1181). WAVES_DIRP에서 Dwavep를 avgWdip로 복사한다(1182–1194). 각 블록은 별도 Aout 조건을 가지며 rho 위치에서 WET_DRY이면 rmask_full을 곱한다. 방향(direction) 값을 이 초기화 블록에서 삼각함수 성분으로 바꾸는 대입은 없다. 원문: `# ifdef WAVES_LENGTHP` (1156); `IF (Aout(idWlep,ng)) THEN` (1157); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1158); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1159); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgWlep(i,j)=FORCES(ng)%Lwavep(i,j)` (1160); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (1161); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgWlep(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgWlep(i,j)*        &` (1162); `&                                 GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (1163); `#  endif` (1164); `# endif` (1168); `# ifdef WAVES_DIR` (1169); `IF (Aout(idWdir,ng)) THEN` (1170); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1171); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1172); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgWdir(i,j)=FORCES(ng)%Dwave(i,j)` (1173); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (1174); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgWdir(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgWdir(i,j)*        &` (1175); `&                                 GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (1176); `#  endif` (1177); `# endif` (1181); `# ifdef WAVES_DIRP` (1182); `IF (Aout(idWdip,ng)) THEN` (1183); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1184); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1185); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgWdip(i,j)=FORCES(ng)%Dwavep(i,j)` (1186); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (1187); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgWdip(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgWdip(i,j)*        &` (1188); `&                                 GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (1189); `#  endif` (1190); `# endif` (1194). |
| 1195–1233 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·118행 set_avg_tile·252–255행 초기화 IF 안. WAVES_TOP_PERIOD와 WAVES_BOT_PERIOD에서 Pwave_top·Pwave_bot을 avgWptp·avgWpbt로 복사한다(1195–1220). BBL_MODEL에서 파랑 궤도 속도(wave orbital velocity) Uwave_rms를 avgWorb로 복사한다(1221–1233). 각 블록은 출력 선택을 검사하며 rho 위치에서 WET_DRY일 때 rmask_full을 곱한다. 원문: `# ifdef WAVES_TOP_PERIOD` (1195); `IF (Aout(idWptp,ng)) THEN` (1196); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1197); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1198); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgWptp(i,j)=FORCES(ng)%Pwave_top(i,j)` (1199); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (1200); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgWptp(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgWptp(i,j)*        &` (1201); `&                                 GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (1202); `#  endif` (1203); `# endif` (1207); `# ifdef WAVES_BOT_PERIOD` (1208); `IF (Aout(idWpbt,ng)) THEN` (1209); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1210); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1211); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgWpbt(i,j)=FORCES(ng)%Pwave_bot(i,j)` (1212); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (1213); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgWpbt(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgWpbt(i,j)*        &` (1214); `&                                 GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (1215); `#  endif` (1216); `# endif` (1220); `# ifdef BBL_MODEL` (1221); `IF (Aout(idWorb,ng)) THEN` (1222); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1223); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1224); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgWorb(i,j)=FORCES(ng)%Uwave_rms(i,j)` (1225); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (1226); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgWorb(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgWorb(i,j)*        &` (1227); `&                                 GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (1228); `#  endif` (1229); `# endif` (1233). |
| 1234–1271 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·118행 set_avg_tile·252–255행 초기화 IF 안. WAV_COUPLING 또는 WEC_VF와 BOTTOM_STREAMING 조합에서 Dissip_fric을 avgWdif로 복사한다(1234–1246). WAV_COUPLING·TKE_WAVEDISS·WDISS_THORGUZA·WDISS_CHURTHOR 중 하나가 정의되면 Dissip_break·Dissip_wcap을 avgWdib·avgWdiw로 복사한다(1247–1271). 1247–1248행은 역슬래시로 이어지는 하나의 cpp 조건이다. 각 Aout 분기는 rho 위치에서 WET_DRY이면 rmask_full을 곱한다. 원문: `# if defined WAV_COUPLING \|\| (defined WEC_VF && defined BOTTOM_STREAMING)` (1234); `IF (Aout(idWdif,ng)) THEN` (1235); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1236); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1237); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgWdif(i,j)=FORCES(ng)%Dissip_fric(i,j)` (1238); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (1239); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgWdif(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgWdif(i,j)*        &` (1240); `&                                 GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (1241); `#  endif` (1242); `# endif` (1246); `# if defined WAV_COUPLING \|\| defined TKE_WAVEDISS \|\| \` (1247); `defined WDISS_THORGUZA \|\| defined WDISS_CHURTHOR` (1248); `IF (Aout(idWdib,ng)) THEN` (1249); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1250); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1251); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgWdib(i,j)=FORCES(ng)%Dissip_break(i,j)` (1252); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (1253); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgWdib(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgWdib(i,j)*        &` (1254); `&                                 GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (1255); `#  endif` (1256); `IF (Aout(idWdiw,ng)) THEN` (1260); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1261); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1262); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgWdiw(i,j)=FORCES(ng)%Dissip_wcap(i,j)` (1263); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (1264); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgWdiw(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgWdiw(i,j)*        &` (1265); `&                                 GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (1266); `#  endif` (1267); `# endif` (1271). |
| 1272–1332 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·118행 set_avg_tile·252–255행 초기화 IF 안. ROLLER_SVENDSEN에서 Wave_break를 avgWbrk로 복사한다(1272–1284). WEC_ROLLER에서 쇄파 롤러(roller) Dissip_roller·rollA를 avgWdis·avgWrol로 복사한다(1285–1308). UV_KIRBY에서 uWave·vWave를 avgUwav·avgVwav로 복사한다(1309–1332). 각 선택별 초기화는 rho 위치와 WET_DRY의 rmask_full을 사용한다. 원문: `# ifdef ROLLER_SVENDSEN` (1272); `IF (Aout(idWbrk,ng)) THEN` (1273); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1274); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1275); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgWbrk(i,j)=FORCES(ng)%Wave_break(i,j)` (1276); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (1277); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgWbrk(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgWbrk(i,j)*        &` (1278); `&                                 GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (1279); `#  endif` (1280); `# endif` (1284); `# ifdef WEC_ROLLER` (1285); `IF (Aout(idWdis,ng)) THEN` (1286); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1287); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1288); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgWdis(i,j)=FORCES(ng)%Dissip_roller(i,j)` (1289); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (1290); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgWdis(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgWdis(i,j)*        &` (1291); `&                                 GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (1292); `#  endif` (1293); `IF (Aout(idWrol,ng)) THEN` (1297); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1298); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1299); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgWrol(i,j)=FORCES(ng)%rollA(i,j)` (1300); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (1301); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgWrol(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgWrol(i,j)*        &` (1302); `&                                 GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (1303); `#  endif` (1304); `# endif` (1308); `# ifdef UV_KIRBY` (1309); `IF (Aout(idUwav,ng)) THEN` (1310); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1311); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1312); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgUwav(i,j)=OCEAN(ng)%uWave(i,j)` (1313); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (1314); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgUwav(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgUwav(i,j)*        &` (1315); `&                                 GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (1316); `#  endif` (1317); `IF (Aout(idVwav,ng)) THEN` (1321); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1322); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1323); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgVwav(i,j)=OCEAN(ng)%vWave(i,j)` (1324); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (1325); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgVwav(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgVwav(i,j)*        &` (1326); `&                                 GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (1327); `#  endif` (1328); `# endif` (1332). |
| 1333–1387 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·118행 set_avg_tile·252–255행 초기화 IF 안. 2차원 와도 출력 선택별로 potvor_bar·relvor_bar를 avgpvor2d·avgrvor2d로 복사한다(1336–1357). SOLVE3D이면 k=1..N(ng)에서 potvor·relvor를 avgpvor3d·avgrvor3d로 복사한다(1358–1387). 네 블록은 Jstr:Jend·Istr:Iend를 순회하며 WET_DRY에서 pmask_full을 곱한다. 원문: `IF (Aout(id2dPV,ng)) THEN` (1336); `DO j=Jstr,Jend` (1337); `DO i=Istr,Iend` (1338); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgpvor2d(i,j)=potvor_bar(i,j)` (1339); `# ifdef WET_DRY` (1340); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgpvor2d(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgpvor2d(i,j)*    &` (1341); `&                                   GRID(ng)%pmask_full(i,j)` (1342); `# endif` (1343); `IF (Aout(id2dRV,ng)) THEN` (1347); `DO j=Jstr,Jend` (1348); `DO i=Istr,Iend` (1349); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgrvor2d(i,j)=relvor_bar(i,j)` (1350); `# ifdef WET_DRY` (1351); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgrvor2d(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgrvor2d(i,j)*    &` (1352); `&                                   GRID(ng)%pmask_full(i,j)` (1353); `# endif` (1354); `# ifdef SOLVE3D` (1358); `IF (Aout(id3dPV,ng)) THEN` (1359); `DO k=1,N(ng)` (1360); `DO j=Jstr,Jend` (1361); `DO i=Istr,Iend` (1362); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgpvor3d(i,j,k)=potvor(i,j,k)` (1363); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (1364); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgpvor3d(i,j,k)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgpvor3d(i,j, &` (1365); `&                                                             k)*  &` (1366); `&                                       GRID(ng)%pmask_full(i,j)` (1367); `#  endif` (1368); `IF (Aout(id3dRV,ng)) THEN` (1373); `DO k=1,N(ng)` (1374); `DO j=Jstr,Jend` (1375); `DO i=Istr,Iend` (1376); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgrvor3d(i,j,k)=relvor(i,j,k)` (1377); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (1378); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgrvor3d(i,j,k)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgrvor3d(i,j, &` (1379); `&                                                             k)*  &` (1380); `&                                       GRID(ng)%pmask_full(i,j)` (1381); `#  endif` (1382); `# endif` (1387). |
| 1388–1427 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·118행 set_avg_tile·252–255행 초기화 IF 안. 제곱 필드(quadratic fields) 초기화 주석이 있다(1389). 각 Aout 선택에서 zeta(Kout)·ubar(Kout)·vbar(Kout)의 자기 곱을 avgZZ·avgU2·avgV2에 저장한다(1391–1426). WET_DRY이면 각 rho/u/v full 마스크를 한 번 곱한다. 원문: `IF (Aout(idZZav,ng)) THEN` (1391); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1392); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1393); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgZZ(i,j)=OCEAN(ng)%zeta(i,j,Kout)*          &` (1394); `&                               OCEAN(ng)%zeta(i,j,Kout)` (1395); `# ifdef WET_DRY` (1396); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgZZ(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgZZ(i,j)*            &` (1397); `&                               GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (1398); `# endif` (1399); `IF (Aout(idU2av,ng)) THEN` (1403); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1404); `DO i=Istr,IendR` (1405); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgU2(i,j)=OCEAN(ng)%ubar(i,j,Kout)*          &` (1406); `&                               OCEAN(ng)%ubar(i,j,Kout)` (1407); `# ifdef WET_DRY` (1408); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgU2(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgU2(i,j)*            &` (1409); `&                               GRID(ng)%umask_full(i,j)` (1410); `# endif` (1411); `IF (Aout(idV2av,ng)) THEN` (1415); `DO j=Jstr,JendR` (1416); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1417); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgV2(i,j)=OCEAN(ng)%vbar(i,j,Kout)*          &` (1418); `&                               OCEAN(ng)%vbar(i,j,Kout)` (1419); `# ifdef WET_DRY` (1420); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgV2(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgV2(i,j)*            &` (1421); `&                               GRID(ng)%vmask_full(i,j)` (1422); `# endif` (1423). |
| 1428–1474 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·118행 set_avg_tile·252–255행 초기화 IF 안. 1428행 SOLVE3D에서 각 출력 선택별로 Nout의 u·v 자기 곱을 avgUU·avgVV에 저장한다(1429–1456). idUVav 분기는 0.25_r8에 인접 u 두 값의 합과 인접 v 두 값의 합을 곱하여 avgUV를 초기화한다(1457–1473). 모두 k=1..N(ng)이며 avgUV의 수평 범위는 Jstr:Jend·Istr:Iend이다. WET_DRY에서 avgUU·avgVV·avgUV에는 각각 umask_full·vmask_full·rmask_full을 곱한다. 원문: `# ifdef SOLVE3D` (1428); `IF (Aout(idUUav,ng)) THEN` (1429); `DO k=1,N(ng)` (1430); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1431); `DO i=Istr,IendR` (1432); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgUU(i,j,k)=OCEAN(ng)%u(i,j,k,Nout)*       &` (1433); `&                                   OCEAN(ng)%u(i,j,k,Nout)` (1434); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (1435); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgUU(i,j,k)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgUU(i,j,k)*      &` (1436); `&                                   GRID(ng)%umask_full(i,j)` (1437); `#  endif` (1438); `IF (Aout(idVVav,ng)) THEN` (1443); `DO k=1,N(ng)` (1444); `DO j=Jstr,JendR` (1445); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1446); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgVV(i,j,k)=OCEAN(ng)%v(i,j,k,Nout)*       &` (1447); `&                                   OCEAN(ng)%v(i,j,k,Nout)` (1448); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (1449); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgVV(i,j,k)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgVV(i,j,k)*      &` (1450); `&                                   GRID(ng)%vmask_full(i,j)` (1451); `#  endif` (1452); `IF (Aout(idUVav,ng)) THEN` (1457); `DO k=1,N(ng)` (1458); `DO j=Jstr,Jend` (1459); `DO i=Istr,Iend` (1460); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgUV(i,j,k)=0.25_r8*                       &` (1461); `&                                   (OCEAN(ng)%u(i  ,j  ,k,Nout)+  &` (1462); `&                                    OCEAN(ng)%u(i+1,j  ,k,Nout))* &` (1463); `&                                   (OCEAN(ng)%v(i  ,j  ,k,Nout)+  &` (1464); `&                                    OCEAN(ng)%v(i  ,j+1,k,Nout))` (1465); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (1466); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgUV(i,j,k)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgUV(i,j,k)*      &` (1467); `&                                   GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (1468); `#  endif` (1469). |
| 1475–1501 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·1428행 `# ifdef SOLVE3D`·118행 set_avg_tile·252–255행 초기화 IF 안. idHUav·idHVav 출력 선택에서 GRID의 Huon·Hvom을 avgHuon·avgHvom에 복사한다(1475–1500). k=1..N(ng)에서 u/v 위치를 각각 순회하며 WET_DRY에서 umask_full·vmask_full을 곱한다. 원문: `IF (Aout(idHUav,ng)) THEN` (1475); `DO k=1,N(ng)` (1476); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1477); `DO i=Istr,IendR` (1478); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgHuon(i,j,k)=GRID(ng)%Huon(i,j,k)` (1479); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (1480); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgHuon(i,j,k)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgHuon(i,j,k)*  &` (1481); `&                                     GRID(ng)%umask_full(i,j)` (1482); `#  endif` (1483); `IF (Aout(idHVav,ng)) THEN` (1488); `DO k=1,N(ng)` (1489); `DO j=Jstr,JendR` (1490); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1491); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgHvom(i,j,k)=GRID(ng)%Hvom(i,j,k)` (1492); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (1493); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgHvom(i,j,k)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgHvom(i,j,k)*  &` (1494); `&                                     GRID(ng)%vmask_full(i,j)` (1495); `#  endif` (1496). |
| 1502–1558 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·1428행 `# ifdef SOLVE3D`·118행 set_avg_tile·252–255행 초기화 IF 안. it=1..NT(ng) 루프를 연다(1502). idTTav(it) 선택은 t(Nout,it)의 자기 곱을 avgTT에 저장한다(1503–1519). idUTav(it)는 0.5_r8·u(Nout)와 i-1/i 두 추적자 값의 합을 곱하여 avgUT를 만든다(1520–1538). idVTav(it)는 0.5_r8·v(Nout)와 j-1/j 두 추적자 값의 합으로 avgVT를 만든다(1539–1557). WET_DRY에서 각각 rho/u/v full 마스크를 곱한다. UT의 i 범위는 Istr:Iend이며 VT의 j 범위는 Jstr:Jend이다. 원문: `DO it=1,NT(ng)` (1502); `IF (Aout(idTTav(it),ng)) THEN` (1503); `DO k=1,N(ng)` (1504); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1505); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1506); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgTT(i,j,k,it)=OCEAN(ng)%t(i,j,k,        &` (1507); `&                                                    Nout,it)*     &` (1508); `&                                        OCEAN(ng)%t(i,j,k,        &` (1509); `&                                                    Nout,it)` (1510); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (1511); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgTT(i,j,k,it)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgTT(i,j,k,  &` (1512); `&                                                          it)*    &` (1513); `&                                        GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)` (1514); `#  endif` (1515); `IF (Aout(idUTav(it),ng)) THEN` (1520); `DO k=1,N(ng)` (1521); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1522); `DO i=Istr,Iend` (1523); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgUT(i,j,k,it)=0.5_r8*                   &` (1524); `&                                        OCEAN(ng)%u(i,j,k,Nout)*  &` (1525); `&                                        (OCEAN(ng)%t(i-1,j,k,     &` (1526); `&                                                     Nout,it)+    &` (1527); `&                                         OCEAN(ng)%t(i  ,j,k,     &` (1528); `&                                                     Nout,it))` (1529); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (1530); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgUT(i,j,k,it)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgUT(i,j,k,  &` (1531); `&                                                          it)*    &` (1532); `&                                        GRID(ng)%umask_full(i,j)` (1533); `#  endif` (1534); `IF (Aout(idVTav(it),ng)) THEN` (1539); WET_DRY이면 pmask_avg·rmask_avg·umask_avg·vmask_avg에 full 마스크를 0.0_r8..1.0_r8로 제한한 값을 더한다(1608–1646). 네 격자별 카운터(counter)는 초기화 때와 같은 수평 범위를 사용한다. 상태 변수 누적 주석이 이어진다(1648–1649). 원문: `ELSE IF (iic(ng).gt.ntsAVG(ng)) THEN` (1606); `# ifdef WET_DRY` (1608); `DO j=Jstr,JendR` (1614); `DO i=Istr,IendR` (1615); `GRID(ng)%pmask_avg(i,j)=GRID(ng)%pmask_avg(i,j)+            &` (1616); `&                              MAX(0.0_r8,                         &` (1617); `&                                  MIN(GRID(ng)%pmask_full(i,j),   &` (1618); `&                                      1.0_r8))` (1619); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1622); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1623); `GRID(ng)%rmask_avg(i,j)=GRID(ng)%rmask_avg(i,j)+            &` (1624); `&                              MAX(0.0_r8,                         &` (1625); `&                                  MIN(GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j),   &` (1626); `&                                      1.0_r8))` (1627); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1630); `DO i=Istr,IendR` (1631); `GRID(ng)%umask_avg(i,j)=GRID(ng)%umask_avg(i,j)+            &` (1632); `&                              MAX(0.0_r8,                         &` (1633); `&                                  MIN(GRID(ng)%umask_full(i,j),   &` (1634); `&                                      1.0_r8))` (1635); `DO j=Jstr,JendR` (1638); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1639); `GRID(ng)%vmask_avg(i,j)=GRID(ng)%vmask_avg(i,j)+            &` (1640); `&                              MAX(0.0_r8,                         &` (1641); `&                                  MIN(GRID(ng)%vmask_full(i,j),   &` (1642); `&                                      1.0_r8))` (1643); `# endif` (1646). |

WET_DRY이면 추가할 값에 각각 rho/u/v full 마스크를 곱한다. 동쪽·북쪽 2차원 속도 출력이 모두 선택되면 uv_rotate2d를 .TRUE., .FALSE.로 호출한다(1685–1697). MASKING은 rmask_full을 전달한다. 원문: `IF (Aout(idFsur,ng)) THEN` (1650); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1651); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1652); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgzeta(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgzeta(i,j)+        &` (1653); `# ifdef WET_DRY` (1654); `&                                 GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)*        &` (1655); `# endif` (1656); `&                                 OCEAN(ng)%zeta(i,j,Kout)` (1657); `IF (Aout(idUbar,ng)) THEN` (1662); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1663); `DO i=Istr,IendR` (1664); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgu2d(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgu2d(i,j)+          &` (1665); `# ifdef WET_DRY` (1666); `&                                GRID(ng)%umask_full(i,j)*         &` (1667); `# endif` (1668); `&                                OCEAN(ng)%ubar(i,j,Kout)` (1669); `IF (Aout(idVbar,ng)) THEN` (1673); `DO j=Jstr,JendR` (1674); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1675); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgv2d(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgv2d(i,j)+          &` (1676); `# ifdef WET_DRY` (1677); `&                                GRID(ng)%vmask_full(i,j)*         &` (1678); `# endif` (1679); `&                                OCEAN(ng)%vbar(i,j,Kout)` (1680); `IF (Aout(idu2dE,ng).and.Aout(idv2dN,ng)) THEN` (1685); `CALL uv_rotate2d (ng, tile, .TRUE., .FALSE.,                  &` (1686); `&                      LBi, UBi, LBj, UBj,                         &` (1687); `&                      GRID(ng) % CosAngler,                       &` (1688); `&                      GRID(ng) % SinAngler,                       &` (1689); `# ifdef MASKING` (1690); `&                      GRID(ng)%rmask_full,                        &` (1691); `# endif` (1692); `&                      OCEAN(ng) % ubar(:,:,Kout),                 &` (1693); `&                      OCEAN(ng) % vbar(:,:,Kout),                 &` (1694); `&                      AVERAGE(ng)%avgu2dE,                        &` (1695); `&                      AVERAGE(ng)%avgv2dN)` (1696). |

WET_DRY는 격자 방향 u와 동쪽 ua에 umask_full, 격자 방향 v와 북쪽 va에 vmask_full을 곱한다. ua·va의 수평 범위는 모두 JstrR:JendR·IstrR:IendR이다. 원문: `# ifdef SOLVE3D` (1699); `IF (Aout(idUvel,ng)) THEN` (1700); `DO k=1,N(ng)` (1701); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1702); `DO i=Istr,IendR` (1703); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgu3d(i,j,k)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgu3d(i,j,k)+    &` (1704); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (1705); `&                                    GRID(ng)%umask_full(i,j)*     &` (1706); `#  endif` (1707); `&                                    OCEAN(ng)%u(i,j,k,Nout)` (1708); `IF (Aout(idVvel,ng)) THEN` (1713); `DO k=1,N(ng)` (1714); `DO j=Jstr,JendR` (1715); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1716); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgv3d(i,j,k)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgv3d(i,j,k)+    &` (1717); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (1718); `&                                    GRID(ng)%vmask_full(i,j)*     &` (1719); `#  endif` (1720); `&                                    OCEAN(ng)%v(i,j,k,Nout)` (1721); `IF (Aout(idu3dE,ng)) THEN` (1727); `DO k=1,N(ng)` (1728); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1729); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1730); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgu3dE(i,j,k)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgu3dE(i,j,k)+  &` (1731); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (1732); `&                                     GRID(ng)%umask_full(i,j)*    &` (1733); `#  endif` (1734); `&                                     OCEAN(ng)%ua(i,j,k)` (1735); `IF (Aout(idv3dN,ng)) THEN` (1740); `DO k=1,N(ng)` (1741); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1742); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1743); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgv3dN(i,j,k)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgv3dN(i,j,k)+  &` (1744); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (1745); `&                                    GRID(ng)%vmask_full(i,j)*     &` (1746); `#  endif` (1747); `&                                    OCEAN(ng)%va(i,j,k)` (1748). |

SEDIMENT와 BEDLOAD에서 it=1..NST를 순회한다(1812–1813). idUbld(it)·idVbld(it) 선택에 따라 기존 avgbedldu·avgbedldv에 bedldu·bedldv를 더한다(1814–1838). WET_DRY에서 추가하는 u/v 값에 각각 umask_full·vmask_full을 곱한다. 1839행은 it 루프를 닫고 1840행은 퇴적물 전처리 조건을 닫는다. 원문: `#  if defined SEDIMENT && defined BEDLOAD` (1812); `DO it=1,NST` (1813); `IF (Aout(idUbld(it),ng)) THEN` (1814); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1815); `DO i=Istr,IendR` (1816); `SEDBED(ng)%avgbedldu(i,j,it)=SEDBED(ng)%avgbedldu(i,j,  &` (1817); `&                                                            it)+  &` (1818); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (1819); `&                                       GRID(ng)%umask_full(i,j)*  &` (1820); `#   endif` (1821); `&                                       SEDBED(ng)%bedldu(i,j,it)` (1822); `IF (Aout(idVbld(it),ng)) THEN` (1827); `DO j=Jstr,JendR` (1828); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1829); `SEDBED(ng)%avgbedldv(i,j,it)=SEDBED(ng)%avgbedldv(i,j,  &` (1830); `&                                                            it)+  &` (1831); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (1832); `&                                       GRID(ng)%vmask_full(i,j)*  &` (1833); `#   endif` (1834); `&                                       SEDBED(ng)%bedldv(i,j,it)` (1835); `#  endif` (1840). |

LMD_MIXING·MY25_MIXING·GLS_MIXING 중 하나가 정의되면 출력 선택별로 기존 avgAKv·avgAKt에 Akv·Akt(itemp)를 더한다(1842–1868). SALINITY이면 avgAKs에 Akt(isalt)를 더한다(1869–1883). 세 블록은 k=0..N(ng) 및 rho 위치 범위에서 WET_DRY일 때 rmask_full을 추가 값에 곱한다. 1884행은 혼합 조건을 닫는다. 원문: `#  if defined LMD_MIXING \|\| defined MY25_MIXING \|\| defined GLS_MIXING` (1842); `IF (Aout(idVvis,ng)) THEN` (1843); `DO k=0,N(ng)` (1844); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1845); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1846); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgAKv(i,j,k)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgAKv(i,j,k)+    &` (1847); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (1848); `&                                    GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)*     &` (1849); `#   endif` (1850); `&                                    MIXING(ng)%Akv(i,j,k)` (1851); `IF (Aout(idTdif,ng)) THEN` (1856); `DO k=0,N(ng)` (1857); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1858); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1859); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgAKt(i,j,k)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgAKt(i,j,k)+    &` (1860); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (1861); `&                                    GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)*     &` (1862); `#   endif` (1863); `&                                    MIXING(ng)%Akt(i,j,k,itemp)` (1864); `#   ifdef SALINITY` (1869); `IF (Aout(idSdif,ng)) THEN` (1870); `DO k=0,N(ng)` (1871); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1872); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1873); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgAKs(i,j,k)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgAKs(i,j,k)+    &` (1874); `#    ifdef WET_DRY` (1875); `&                                    GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)*     &` (1876); `#    endif` (1877); `&                                    MIXING(ng)%Akt(i,j,k,isalt)` (1878); `#   endif` (1883); `#  endif` (1884). |

LMD_BKPP에서 idHbbl이 선택되면 avghbbl에 hbbl을 더한다(1898–1910). 두 분기는 rho 위치 범위에서 WET_DRY이면 추가 값에 rmask_full을 곱한다. 1911행은 1699행 SOLVE3D를 닫는다. 원문: `#  ifdef LMD_SKPP` (1885); `IF (Aout(idHsbl,ng)) THEN` (1886); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1887); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1888); `AVERAGE(ng)%avghsbl(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avghsbl(i,j)+        &` (1889); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (1890); `&                                 GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)*        &` (1891); `#   endif` (1892); `&                                 MIXING(ng)%hsbl(i,j)` (1893); `#  endif` (1897); `#  ifdef LMD_BKPP` (1898); `IF (Aout(idHbbl,ng)) THEN` (1899); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1900); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1901); `AVERAGE(ng)%avghbbl(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avghbbl(i,j)+        &` (1902); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (1903); `&                                 GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)*        &` (1904); `#   endif` (1905); `&                                 MIXING(ng)%hbbl(i,j)` (1906); `#  endif` (1910); `# endif` (1911). |
| 1913–1965 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·118행 set_avg_tile·1606행 누적 ELSE IF(252–255행 IF의 대안 분기) 안. FORWARD_WRITE와 SOLVE3D에서 DU_avg1·DU_avg2·DV_avg1·DV_avg2를 출력 선택별 기존 결합 평균에 더한다(1913–1962). WET_DRY이면 DU에 umask_full, DV에 vmask_full을 곱한다. 표면·바닥 플럭스 누적 주석이 뒤따른다(1964–1965). 원문: `# if defined FORWARD_WRITE && defined SOLVE3D` (1913); `IF (Aout(idUfx1,ng)) THEN` (1917); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1918); `DO i=Istr,IendR` (1919); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgDU_avg1(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgDU_avg1(i,j)+  &` (1920); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (1921); `&                                    GRID(ng)%umask_full(i,j)*     &` (1922); `#  endif` (1923); `&                                    COUPLING(ng)%DU_avg1(i,j)` (1924); `IF (Aout(idUfx2,ng)) THEN` (1928); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1929); `DO i=Istr,IendR` (1930); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgDU_avg2(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgDU_avg2(i,j)+  &` (1931); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (1932); `&                                    GRID(ng)%umask_full(i,j)*     &` (1933); `#  endif` (1934); `&                                    COUPLING(ng)%DU_avg2(i,j)` (1935); `IF (Aout(idVfx1,ng)) THEN` (1940); `DO j=Jstr,JendR` (1941); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1942); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgDV_avg1(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgDV_avg1(i,j)+  &` (1943); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (1944); `&                                    GRID(ng)%vmask_full(i,j)*     &` (1945); `#  endif` (1946); `&                                    COUPLING(ng)%DV_avg1(i,j)` (1947); `IF (Aout(idVfx2,ng)) THEN` (1951); `DO j=Jstr,JendR` (1952); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1953); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgDV_avg2(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgDV_avg2(i,j)+  &` (1954); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (1955); `&                                    GRID(ng)%vmask_full(i,j)*     &` (1956); `#  endif` (1957); `&                                    COUPLING(ng)%DV_avg2(i,j)` (1958); `# endif` (1962). |
| 1966–1999 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·118행 set_avg_tile·1606행 누적 ELSE IF(252–255행 IF의 대안 분기) 안. idUsms·idVsms·idUbms 선택에 따라 기존 avgsus·avgsvs·avgbus에 sustr·svstr·bustr를 더한다(1966–1999). WET_DRY에서 추가 값에 u/v/u full 마스크를 각각 곱한다. 2000행의 다음 출력 선택 분기는 이 구간 밖이다. 원문: `IF (Aout(idUsms,ng)) THEN` (1966); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1967); `DO i=Istr,IendR` (1968); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgsus(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgsus(i,j)+          &` (1969); `# ifdef WET_DRY` (1970); `&                                GRID(ng)%umask_full(i,j)*         &` (1971); `# endif` (1972); `&                                FORCES(ng)%sustr(i,j)` (1973); `IF (Aout(idVsms,ng)) THEN` (1977); `DO j=Jstr,JendR` (1978); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (1979); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgsvs(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgsvs(i,j)+          &` (1980); `# ifdef WET_DRY` (1981); `&                                GRID(ng)%vmask_full(i,j)*         &` (1982); `# endif` (1983); `&                                FORCES(ng)%svstr(i,j)` (1984); `IF (Aout(idUbms,ng)) THEN` (1989); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (1990); `DO i=Istr,IendR` (1991); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgbus(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgbus(i,j)+          &` (1992); `# ifdef WET_DRY` (1993); `&                                GRID(ng)%umask_full(i,j)*         &` (1994); `# endif` (1995); `&                                FORCES(ng)%bustr(i,j)` (1996). |
| 2000–2044 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·118행 set_avg_tile·1606행 누적 ELSE IF 안. idVbms 출력 선택에서 avgbvs에 bvstr를 더하며 WET_DRY이면 vmask_full을 곱한다(2000–2010). 2011행은 `# ifdef BBL`이다. idUbrs·idVbrs·idUbws별로 FORCES의 bustrc·bvstrc·bustrw를 기존 평균에 더한다(2012–2044). 이 세 BBL 누적 블록은 rho 위치 범위와 WET_DRY의 rmask_full을 사용한다. 원문: `IF (Aout(idVbms,ng)) THEN` (2000); `DO j=Jstr,JendR` (2001); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (2002); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgbvs(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgbvs(i,j)+          &` (2003); `# ifdef WET_DRY` (2004); `&                                GRID(ng)%vmask_full(i,j)*         &` (2005); `# endif` (2006); `&                                FORCES(ng)%bvstr(i,j)` (2007); `# ifdef BBL` (2011); `IF (Aout(idUbrs,ng)) THEN` (2012); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (2013); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (2014); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgUbrs(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgUbrs(i,j)+        &` (2015); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (2016); `&                                 GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)*        &` (2017); `#  endif` (2018); `&                                 FORCES(ng)%bustrc(i,j)` (2019); `IF (Aout(idVbrs,ng)) THEN` (2023); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (2024); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (2025); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgVbrs(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgVbrs(i,j)+        &` (2026); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (2027); `&                                 GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)*        &` (2028); `#  endif` (2029); `&                                 FORCES(ng)%bvstrc(i,j)` (2030); `IF (Aout(idUbws,ng)) THEN` (2034); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (2035); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (2036); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgUbws(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgUbws(i,j)+        &` (2037); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (2038); `&                                GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)*         &` (2039); `#  endif` (2040); `&                                FORCES(ng)%bustrw(i,j)` (2041). |
| 2045–2077 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·2011행 `# ifdef BBL`·118행 set_avg_tile·1606행 누적 ELSE IF 안. idVbws·idUbcs·idVbcs 선택별로 FORCES의 bvstrw·bustrcwmax·bvstrcwmax를 각각 avgVbws·avgUbcs·avgVbcs에 더한다(2045–2077). 세 블록은 rho 위치 범위에서 WET_DRY이면 추가 값에 rmask_full을 곱한다. 원문: `IF (Aout(idVbws,ng)) THEN` (2045); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (2046); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (2047); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgVbws(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgVbws(i,j)+        &` (2048); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (2049); `&                                GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)*         &` (2050); `#  endif` (2051); `&                                FORCES(ng)%bvstrw(i,j)` (2052); `IF (Aout(idUbcs,ng)) THEN` (2056); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (2057); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (2058); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgUbcs(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgUbcs(i,j)+        &` (2059); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (2060); `&                                GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)*         &` (2061); `#  endif` (2062); `&                                FORCES(ng)%bustrcwmax(i,j)` (2063); `IF (Aout(idVbcs,ng)) THEN` (2067); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (2068); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (2069); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgVbcs(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgVbcs(i,j)+        &` (2070); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (2071); `&                                GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)*         &` (2072); `#  endif` (2073); `&                                FORCES(ng)%bvstrcwmax(i,j)` (2074). |
| 2078–2138 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·2011행 `# ifdef BBL`·118행 set_avg_tile·1606행 누적 ELSE IF 안. idUVwc 선택은 wrk를 할당하고 BBL의 최대 복합 응력 두 성분의 제곱합에 1.0E-10_r8을 더한 제곱근을 계산하여 avgUVwc에 더한 후 해제한다(2078–2093). Ubot·Vbot·Ubur·Vbur를 출력 선택별 avgUbot·avgVbot·avgUbur·avgVbvr에 더한다(2094–2137). 다섯 평균 블록은 rho 위치 범위와 WET_DRY의 rmask_full을 사용한다. 2138행은 BBL을 닫는다. 원문: `IF (Aout(idUVwc,ng)) THEN` (2078); `wrk(LBi:UBi,LBj:UBj)=0.0_r8` (2080); | 2139–2166 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·118행 set_avg_tile·1606행 누적 ELSE IF 안. 2139행 SOLVE3D를 연다. BULK_FLUXES·ECOSIM·ATM_PRESS 중 하나가 정의되면 avgPair에 Pair를 누적한다(2141–2153). BULK_FLUXES이면 avgTair에 Tair를 누적한다(2154–2166). 각 출력 선택별 블록은 rho 위치 범위와 WET_DRY의 rmask_full을 사용한다. 원문: `# ifdef SOLVE3D` (2139); `#  if defined BULK_FLUXES \|\| defined ECOSIM \|\| defined ATM_PRESS` (2141); `IF (Aout(idPair,ng)) THEN` (2142); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (2143); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (2144); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgPair(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgPair(i,j)+        &` (2145); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (2146); `&                                 GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)*        &` (2147); `#   endif` (2148); `&                                 FORCES(ng)%Pair(i,j)` (2149); `#  endif` (2153); `#  if defined BULK_FLUXES` (2154); `IF (Aout(idTair,ng)) THEN` (2155); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (2156); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (2157); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgTair(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgTair(i,j)+        &` (2158); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (2159); `&                                 GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)*        &` (2160); `#   endif` (2161); `&                                 FORCES(ng)%Tair(i,j)` (2162); `#  endif` (2166). |

BULK_FLUXES 또는 ECOSIM에서 avgUwind·avgVwind에 Uwind·Vwind를 각 출력 선택에 따라 더하고 WET_DRY이면 rmask_full을 곱한다(2167–2190). 동쪽·북쪽 바람 출력의 논리곱 조건에서 uv_rotate2d(.TRUE., .FALSE.)를 호출한다(2192–2204). MASKING은 rmask_full을 전달한다. 2205행은 대기 플럭스 전처리 분기를 닫는다. 원문: `#  if defined BULK_FLUXES \|\| defined ECOSIM` (2167); `IF (Aout(idUair,ng)) THEN` (2168); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (2169); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (2170); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgUwind(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgUwind(i,j)+      &` (2171); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (2172); `&                                  GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)*       &` (2173); `#   endif` (2174); `&                                  FORCES(ng)%Uwind(i,j)` (2175); `IF (Aout(idVair,ng)) THEN` (2180); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (2181); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (2182); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgVwind(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgVwind(i,j)+      &` (2183); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (2184); `&                                  GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)*       &` (2185); `#   endif` (2186); `&                                  FORCES(ng)%Vwind(i,j)` (2187); `IF (Aout(idUaiE,ng).and.Aout(idVaiN,ng)) THEN` (2192); `CALL uv_rotate2d (ng, tile, .TRUE., .FALSE.,                  &` (2193); `&                      LBi, UBi, LBj, UBj,                         &` (2194); `&                      GRID(ng) % CosAngler,                       &` (2195); `&                      GRID(ng) % SinAngler,                       &` (2196); `#   ifdef MASKING` (2197); `&                      GRID(ng)%rmask_full,                        &` (2198); `#   endif` (2199); `&                      FORCES(ng) % Uwind,                         &` (2200); `&                      FORCES(ng) % Vwind,                         &` (2201); `&                      AVERAGE(ng)%avgUwindE,                      &` (2202); `&                      AVERAGE(ng)%avgVwindN)` (2203); `#  endif` (2205). |

SHORTWAVE에서 avgsrf에 srflx를 누적한다(2231–2243). 각 출력 선택별 블록은 rho 위치와 WET_DRY의 rmask_full을 사용한다. 원문: `IF (Aout(idTsur(itemp),ng)) THEN` (2207); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (2208); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (2209); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgstf(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgstf(i,j)+          &` (2210); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (2211); `&                                GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)*         &` (2212); `#  endif` (2213); `&                                FORCES(ng)%stflx(i,j,itemp)` (2214); `#  ifdef SALINITY` (2218); `IF (Aout(idTsur(isalt),ng)) THEN` (2219); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (2220); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (2221); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgswf(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgswf(i,j)+          &` (2222); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (2223); `&                                GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)*         &` (2224); `#   endif` (2225); `&                                FORCES(ng)%stflx(i,j,isalt)` (2226); `#  endif` (2230); `#  ifdef SHORTWAVE` (2231); `IF (Aout(idSrad,ng)) THEN` (2232); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (2233); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (2234); `AVERAGE(ng)%avgsrf(i,j)=AVERAGE(ng)%avgsrf(i,j)+          &` (2235); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (2236); `&                                GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)*         &` (2237); `#   endif` (2238); `&                                FORCES(ng)%srflx(i,j)` (2239); `#  endif` (2243). |

WET_DRY에서 pfac/rfac/ufac/vfac는 각 마스크 카운터를 최소 1.0_r8로 제한한 값의 역수이다(2981–2989). #else에서는 fac=1.0_r8/REAL(nAVG(ng),r8)를 네 배열에 복사한다(2990–3000). 네 계수 배열의 수평 범위는 모두 JstrR:JendR·IstrR:IendR이다. 원문: `IF (((iic(ng).gt.ntsAVG(ng)).and.                                 &` (2962); `&     (MOD(iic(ng)-1,nAVG(ng)).eq.0).and.                          &` (2963); `&     ((iic(ng).ne.ntstart(ng)).or.(nrrec(ng).eq.0))).or.          &` (2964); `&    ((iic(ng).ge.ntsAVG(ng)).and.(nAVG(ng).eq.1))) THEN` (2965); `IF (DOMAIN(ng)%SouthWest_Test(tile)) THEN` (2966); `IF (nAVG(ng).eq.1) THEN` (2967); `AVGtime(ng)=time(ng)` (2968); `ELSE` (2969); `AVGtime(ng)=AVGtime(ng)+REAL(nAVG(ng),r8)*dt(ng)` (2970); `# ifdef WET_DRY` (2976); `# endif` (2979); `# ifdef WET_DRY` (2981); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (2982); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (2983); `pfac(i,j)=1.0_r8/MAX(1.0_r8, GRID(ng)%pmask_avg(i,j))` (2984); `rfac(i,j)=1.0_r8/MAX(1.0_r8, GRID(ng)%rmask_avg(i,j))` (2985); `ufac(i,j)=1.0_r8/MAX(1.0_r8, GRID(ng)%umask_avg(i,j))` (2986); `vfac(i,j)=1.0_r8/MAX(1.0_r8, GRID(ng)%vmask_avg(i,j))` (2987); `# else` (2990); `fac=1.0_r8/REAL(nAVG(ng),r8)` (2991); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (2992); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (2993); `pfac(i,j)=fac` (2994); `rfac(i,j)=fac` (2995); `ufac(i,j)=fac` (2996); `vfac(i,j)=fac` (2997); `# endif` (3000). |

| 5190–5251 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·2행 set_avg_mod 안. 5191행은 AVERAGES_DETIDE 및 SSH_TIDES 또는 UV_TIDES 조건을 연다. set_detide_tile의 인수는 격자 경계·NTC·Kout·조건부 Nout과 CosOmega/SinOmega·평균/누적/교차곱 배열이다(5194–5204). mod_param·mod_ncparam·mod_average·mod_ocean·mod_scalars·mod_tides를 사용하며 WET_DRY이면 mod_grid를 사용한다(5207–5215). ASSUMED_SHAPE는 배열 크기를 콜론으로 선언하고 #else에서는 NTC 또는 NTC×NTC를 명시한다(5230–5250). CosOmega·SinOmega는 intent(in), 나머지 조화 배열은 intent(inout)이다. 원문: `# if defined AVERAGES_DETIDE && (defined SSH_TIDES \|\| defined UV_TIDES)` (5191); `SUBROUTINE set_detide_tile (ng, tile,                             &` (5194); `&                            LBi, UBi, LBj, UBj,                   &` (5195); `&                            IminS, ImaxS, JminS, JmaxS,           &` (5196); `&                            NTC, Kout,                            &` (5197); `#  ifdef SOLVE3D` (5198); `&                            Nout,                                 &` (5199); `#  endif` (5200); `&                            CosOmega, SinOmega,                   &` (5201); `&                            CosW_avg, CosW_sum,                   &` (5202); `&                            SinW_avg, SinW_sum,                   &` (5203); `&                            CosWCosW, SinWSinW, SinWCosW)` (5204); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (5210); `#  endif` (5212); `#  ifdef SOLVE3D` (5225); `#  endif` (5227); `#  ifdef ASSUMED_SHAPE` (5230); `#  else` (5240); `#  endif` (5250). |
| 5252–5289 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·5191행 `# if defined AVERAGES_DETIDE && (defined SSH_TIDES \|\| defined UV_TIDES)`·5194행 set_detide_tile 안. 지역 변수와 indx(2*NTC+1), Ak·tide_harmonics의 0:2*NTC 범위, C·Y의 0:2*NTC 정방 행렬, rho/u/v 계수 배열을 선언한다(5254–5268). set_bounds.h를 포함한다(5270). nAVG=0이면 반환한다(5276). NTC2=2*NTC를 계산한다(5284). 5286–5288행 초기화 조건은 평균 시작 이후 MOD(iic-1,nAVG)=1 또는 재시작 첫 단계를 검사한다. 이 조건에는 일반 평균 초기화의 nAVG=1 대안이 없다(252–255와 비교). 원문: `#  include "set_bounds.h"` (5270); `IF (nAVG(ng).eq.0) RETURN` (5276); `NTC2=2*NTC` (5284); `IF (((iic(ng).gt.ntsAVG(ng)).and.                                 &` (5286); `&     (MOD(iic(ng)-1,nAVG(ng)).eq.1)).or.                          &` (5287); `&    ((nrrec(ng).gt.0).and.(iic(ng).eq.ntstart(ng)))) THEN` (5288). |
| 5290–5316 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·5191행 `# if defined AVERAGES_DETIDE && (defined SSH_TIDES \|\| defined UV_TIDES)`·5194행 set_detide_tile·5286–5288행 초기화 IF 안. 최소제곱 계수를 초기화하지 않고 계속 누적하여 조석 적합(tidal fit)을 개선한다는 주석이 있다(5290–5293). SouthWest_Test 타일에서 Hcount를 1 늘리고 nk=1..NTC의 SinW_avg·CosW_avg를 현재 SinOmega·CosOmega로 초기화한다(5295–5299). SinW_sum·CosW_sum과 mk/nk 교차곱 세 배열에는 현재 성분을 더한다(5300–5307). 같은 타일 조건 안에서 tide_harmonics의 0 성분은 1.0_r8, 1..NTC는 SinOmega, NTC+1..2*NTC는 CosOmega로 설정한다(5308–5313). 원문: `IF (DOMAIN(ng)%SouthWest_Test(tile)) THEN` (5295); `Hcount(ng)=Hcount(ng)+1` (5296); `DO nk=1,NTC` (5297); `SinW_avg(nk)=SinOmega(nk)` (5298); `CosW_avg(nk)=CosOmega(nk)` (5299); `SinW_sum(nk)=SinW_sum(nk)+SinOmega(nk)` (5300); `CosW_sum(nk)=CosW_sum(nk)+CosOmega(nk)` (5301); `DO mk=1,NTC` (5302); `SinWSinW(mk,nk)=SinWSinW(mk,nk)+SinOmega(mk)*SinOmega(nk)` (5303); `CosWCosW(mk,nk)=CosWCosW(mk,nk)+CosOmega(mk)*CosOmega(nk)` (5304); `SinWCosW(mk,nk)=SinWCosW(mk,nk)+SinOmega(mk)*CosOmega(nk)` (5305); `tide_harmonics(0)=1.0_r8` (5308); `DO nk=1,NTC` (5309); `tide_harmonics(nk    )=SinOmega(nk)` (5310); `tide_harmonics(nk+NTC)=CosOmega(nk)` (5311). |
| 5317–5364 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·5191행 `# if defined AVERAGES_DETIDE && (defined SSH_TIDES \|\| defined UV_TIDES)`·5194행 set_detide_tile·5286–5288행 초기화 IF 안. 조석 제거 출력 idFsuD·idu2dD·idv2dD 선택별로 nk=0..NTC2를 순회하며 zeta·ubar·vbar와 tide_harmonics(nk)의 곱을 기존 zeta_tide·ubar_tide·vbar_tide에 더한다(5317–5363). 'Initialize' 주석 아래에서도 이 세 대입은 누적형이다. WET_DRY에서 추가 값에 각각 rho/u/v full 마스크를 곱한다. 이 블록들은 5295행 SouthWest_Test IF 바깥이다. 원문: `IF (Aout(idFsuD,ng)) THEN` (5317); `DO nk=0,NTC2` (5318); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (5319); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (5320); `TIDES(ng)%zeta_tide(i,j,nk)=TIDES(ng)%zeta_tide(i,j,    &` (5321); `&                                                          nk)+    &` (5322); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (5323); `&                                      GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)*   &` (5324); `#  endif` (5325); `&                                      OCEAN(ng)%zeta(i,j,Kout)*   &` (5326); `&                                      tide_harmonics(nk)` (5327); `IF (Aout(idu2dD,ng)) THEN` (5333); `DO nk=0,NTC2` (5334); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (5335); `DO i=Istr,IendR` (5336); `TIDES(ng)%ubar_tide(i,j,nk)=TIDES(ng)%ubar_tide(i,j,    &` (5337); `&                                                          nk)+    &` (5338); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (5339); `&                                      GRID(ng)%umask_full(i,j)*   &` (5340); `#  endif` (5341); `&                                      OCEAN(ng)%ubar(i,j,Kout)*   &` (5342); `&                                      tide_harmonics(nk)` (5343); `IF (Aout(idv2dD,ng)) THEN` (5349); `DO nk=0,NTC2` (5350); `DO j=Jstr,JendR` (5351); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (5352); `TIDES(ng)%vbar_tide(i,j,nk)=TIDES(ng)%vbar_tide(i,j,    &` (5353); `&                                                          nk)+    &` (5354); `#  ifdef WET_DRY` (5355); `&                                      GRID(ng)%vmask_full(i,j)*   &` (5356); `#  endif` (5357); `&                                      OCEAN(ng)%vbar(i,j,Kout)*   &` (5358); `&                                      tide_harmonics(nk)` (5359). |
| 5365–5423 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·5191행 `# if defined AVERAGES_DETIDE && (defined SSH_TIDES \|\| defined UV_TIDES)`·5194행 set_detide_tile·5286–5288행 초기화 IF 안. 5365행 SOLVE3D에서 idu3dD·idv3dD 출력 선택별로 nk=0..NTC2·k=1..N(ng)을 순회하여 u/v(Nout)와 tide_harmonics의 곱을 기존 u_tide/v_tide에 더한다(5366–5400). it=1..NAT에서 선택된 추적자와 조화 성분의 곱을 t_tide에 더한다(5402–5421). WET_DRY에서 u/v/t 추가 값에 각각 umask_full·vmask_full·rmask_full을 곱한다. t_tide의 수평 범위는 Jstr:JendR·IstrR:IendR이다. 5422행은 SOLVE3D를 닫는다. 원문: `#  ifdef SOLVE3D` (5365); `IF (Aout(idu3dD,ng)) THEN` (5366); `DO nk=0,NTC2` (5367); `DO k=1,N(ng)` (5368); `DO j=JstrR,JendR` (5369); `DO i=Istr,IendR` (5370); `TIDES(ng)%u_tide(i,j,k,nk)=TIDES(ng)%u_tide(i,j,k,    &` (5371); `&                                                        nk)+      &` (5372); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (5373); `&                                       GRID(ng)%umask_full(i,j)*  &` (5374); `#   endif` (5375); `&                                       OCEAN(ng)%u(i,j,k,Nout)*   &` (5376); `&                                       tide_harmonics(nk)` (5377); `IF (Aout(idv3dD,ng)) THEN` (5384); `DO nk=0,NTC2` (5385); `DO k=1,N(ng)` (5386); `DO j=Jstr,JendR` (5387); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (5388); `TIDES(ng)%v_tide(i,j,k,nk)=TIDES(ng)%v_tide(i,j,k,    &` (5389); `&                                                        nk)+      &` (5390); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (5391); `&                                       GRID(ng)%vmask_full(i,j)*  &` (5392); `#   endif` (5393); `&                                       OCEAN(ng)%v(i,j,k,Nout)*   &` (5394); `&                                       tide_harmonics(nk)` (5395); `DO it=1,NAT` (5402); `IF (Aout(idTrcD(it),ng)) THEN` (5403); `DO nk=0,NTC2` (5404); `DO k=1,N(ng)` (5405); `DO j=Jstr,JendR` (5406); `DO i=IstrR,IendR` (5407); `TIDES(ng)%t_tide(i,j,k,nk,it)=TIDES(ng)%t_tide(i,j, &` (5408); `&                                                        k,nk,it)+ &` (5409); `#   ifdef WET_DRY` (5410); `&                                        GRID(ng)%rmask_full(i,j)* &` (5411); `#   endif` (5412); `&                                            OCEAN(ng)%t(i,j,k,    &` (5413); `&                                                        Nout,it)* &` (5414); `&                                            tide_harmonics(nk)` (5415); `#  endif` (5422). |
| 5424–5454 | 시작 시 3행 `#ifdef AVERAGES`·5191행 `# if defined AVERAGES_DETIDE && (defined SSH_TIDES \|\| defined UV_TIDES)`·5194행 set_detide_tile·5286–5288행 초기화 IF 안. 5428행은 iic가 ntsAVG보다 클 때 실행하는 누적 ELSE IF이다. SouthWest_Test 타일에서 Hcount를 1 늘리고 SinW_avg·CosW_avg·SinW_sum·CosW_sum에 현재 조화 성분을 더한다(5433–5439). mk/nk 교차곱 세 배열에도 현재 곱을 더한다(5440–5445). 같은 타일 조건 안에서 tide_harmonics를 1.0_r8·SinOmega·CosOmega로 설정한다(5446–5451). 조화 주파수 omega가 모델 시간 좌표를 사용한다는 주석은 set_tides를 언급한다(5430–5431). 원문: `ELSE IF (iic(ng).gt.ntsAVG(ng)) THEN` (5428); `IF (DOMAIN(ng)%SouthWest_Test(tile)) THEN` (5433); `Hcount(ng)=Hcount(ng)+1` (5434); `DO nk=1,NTC` (5435); `SinW_avg(nk)=SinW_avg(nk)+SinOmega(nk)` (5436); `CosW_avg(nk)=CosW_avg(nk)+CosOmega(nk)` (5437); `SinW_sum(nk)=SinW_sum(nk)+SinOmega(nk)` (5438); `CosW_sum(nk)=CosW_sum(nk)+CosOmega(nk)` (5439); `DO mk=1,NTC` (5440); `SinWSinW(mk,nk)=SinWSinW(mk,nk)+SinOmega(mk)*SinOmega(nk)` (5441); `CosWCosW(mk,nk)=CosWCosW(mk,nk)+CosOmega(mk)*CosOmega(nk)` (5442); `SinWCosW(mk,nk)=SinWCosW(mk,nk)+SinOmega(mk)*CosOmega(nk)` (5443); `tide_harmonics(0)=1.0_r8` (5446); `DO nk=1,NTC` (5447); `tide_harmonics(nk    )=SinOmega(nk)` (5448); `tide_harmonics(nk+NTC)=CosOmega(nk)` (5449). |
